## Working Practices (FE)
- [**STANDING RULE (2026-07-01): the BACKEND is the SOURCE OF TRUTH — do NOT author backend code. Feature work is FE-only; all PES integration lives in the backend (untouchable — FE only reads pes/authorize + mirrors vocab in FalconAccess). If a feature needs a backend field/endpoint that doesn't exist, FLAG it as blocked-on-backend (needs a backend-team PR), do not add it. Syncing a local checkout to already-merged origin/main + redeploy is OK only when the user explicitly asks ("take the latest from PES"). Triggered by me adding usedInIvr to templates-svc for the Voice lock — reverted.**](feedback_backend_is_sot_do_not_author_backend_2026_07_01.md) — 🔴 STANDING RULE 2026-07-01 (claude).
- [**RULE: never commit/push FE work or create FE branches without explicit user instruction — leave changes UNCOMMITTED on the branch the user names (canonical FE work branch = `polishing-v0.4`) so they can review live. ## Identity / Auth — User Lifecycle (backend)
- [**GSD-005 credential-email login bug FIXED (identity-svc, backend-only, minimal): Pending user whose email/pwd an admin edits couldn't log in with new OR old emailed creds. Cause: `UserCredentialsNotificationHandler` HTML-encodes the password into the email while Zitadel stores it RAW, and `PasswordPolicy.cs:14` special set included `&` → emailed `&amp;` ≠ stored, rejected at Zitadel session PATCH before Pending first-login. FIX = removed `&` from generator set (`"!@#$%^*()_+-="`) so `HtmlEncode` is a no-op for every generated pwd; covers create+unlock+edit (same encoder). Kept firstName/username encoding (XSS). +regression test (2000 gens × 2 levels). build -warnaserror GREEN; 33/33 relevant tests pass; 2 ResendOtp suite fails PRE-EXISTING. UNCOMMITTED; live E2E user-gated.**](project_password_gen_html_encode_login_bug_2026_06_30.md) — 🟢 BACKEND FIXED + BUILD/TESTS GREEN 2026-06-30 (claude); uncommitted, live E2E user-gated.

(2) Pending EMAIL+pwd edit can't login = GSD-005: `UserCredentialsNotificationHandler.cs:59` HtmlEncodes pwd (`&`→`&amp;`) but Zitadel stores raw → drop `&` from `PasswordPolicy.cs:14`. (3) AO edits Active user email/phone → "invalid code length" = profile update auto-verifies/pre-sets contact in Zitadel + marks Mongo unverified so OTP-send resend has no code (length 0, FE-only toast) → defer contact change to the verify flow; edit-lock is FE-only+correct. (4) AO delete → SA sees Active = soft-delete sets only `isDeleted`, never `Status=Deleted`; DTO maps only Status → persist Status=Deleted + backfill. Main used generic slate/emerald/amber; app rebranded to falcon-* → ~10 colors have NO exact falcon token. Decisions (AskUserQuestion ×3): A=faithful lean port (raw-div+Tailwind, NOT falcon-angular-* rebuild); B=true zero-CSS (relocate shimmer, delete scss); C=falcon brand tokens for color (nearest, app-consistent, NO token-lib edit, document deltas). Edits 4 files: html rewritten (radii→main real, skel→`falcon-skel` ×17, colors unchanged); ts (removed styleUrls+getStatusClass); `apps/host-shell/src/tailwind.css` (+`@utility falcon-skel`+`@keyframes`, neutral-token gradient, app-scoped); **DELETED** dashboard.component.scss → zero-CSS. Live visual diff NOT run (rule §8 no-browser-during-impl; user-gated). LESSON: a Tailwind port built on SCSS var()-fallbacks silently drifts from the real theme — resolve the actual token chain; zero-CSS shimmer = `@utility`+`@keyframes`+token-var gradient in app tailwind.css. **+ROUTE FOLLOW-UP:** dashboard wasn't reachable — `app.routes.ts` had DROPPED main's default child `{ path:'', pathMatch:'full', component: DashboardComponent }` (content area is router-driven `<router-outlet>`, no hardcoded `<app-dashboard>`) → `/` rendered empty outlet → blank post-login + sidebar Dashboard (path `/`) click dead. - [**Wallet Balance-Transfer drawer — 4 bugs ROOT-CAUSED + FIXED + LIVE-VERIFIED (drawerSource thrash, NOT the dropdown bridge)** (claude, 2026-06-07, FE-only, NO commits). User bugs: (A) Destination change "doesn't reflect/stick"; (B) locked Destination Wallet never auto-shows in Multiple; (C) "can send to myself"; (D) zero-balance rows' Transfer enabled in some accounts (BMW NodeBased-Single all 0.0000) not others. **ROOT CAUSE (A/B/C, SUPERSEDES the falcon-dropdown-bridge theory which was DISPROVEN live):** orchestrator binds `[source]="store.drawerSource()"` (a METHOD, re-run every CD) and `drawerSource()` built a BRAND-NEW `WbDrawerSource` object literal each call → drawer `source` input identity churns every tick → the constructor (re)initialise effect (`destId.set('')`/`formTouched.set(false)`/`sourceId.set(resolveInitialSourceId)`) RE-FIRES and wipes the user's pick one CD after `pickDest`/`pickSource` ran. Proven live: dest wrapper gains `ng-dirty` (NgModel DID fire) yet formTouched=false + Available stuck on the clicked row → the effect overwrote it; `[attr.value]`+Stencil `.value` both updated → handleChange ran, only drawer signals wiped. **FIX = MEMOIZE `drawerSource()` on the `drawerFor` ref** (stable identity → open-effect runs once → selections persist; B+C fall out of A) in admin `services/wallet.service.ts` + mgmt `wallet-balance-management.component.ts` (kept `drawerSource(): WbDrawerSource {` sig for the source-regex spec). **D = `isRowZero(row)` gate** (Single: getAlloc(row,'single')≤0; Multiple: activeChannels().every ch ≤0) OR-ed into the row Transfer `[disabled]`, admin `wbm-allocation-table` + mgmt `wbm-client-view`. **LIVE (admin DOM-driven, window minimized):** Mercedes NM — Sales→Marketing dest → Destination Wallet APPEARS "Voice·Locked", count 3→4, Cancel enabled, Sales excluded from dest list, Source→Operations Available 2,535→50; BMW NS zeros now disabled (opacity .5, were enabled); Mercedes funded rows ENABLED + zero rows DISABLED (no over-disable). admin build×2 GREEN + `nx test` 837/837 (fixed 3 brittle source-regex assertions, +2 new tests); mgmt build GREEN. ⚠️ mgmt `nx test` BLOCKED by PRE-EXISTING vite `Failed to resolve import "@falcon/ui-core/angular"` (19 unrelated suites; admin resolves it fine) → follow-up. LESSON: a `store.x()` method returning a fresh object, bound as a component `input()`, silently re-fires every effect tracking that input each CD — memoize or use `computed`.](project_wallet_drawer_source_thrash_rootcause_fix_2026_06_07.md) — 🟢 FIXED + LIVE-VERIFIED + builds/admin-tests GREEN 2026-06-07 (claude). **Supersedes** [[project_wallet_dest_wallet_missing_falcon_dropdown_bridge_bug_2026_06_07]]. - [**UI Add-Client account-owner user NEVER created → no login/password** root cause + FIX (2026-06-07, claude, user approved Both). Creating a client via the Add-Client wizard makes the Commerce tenant/node but the owner user is absent from Identity+Zitadel. Owner provisioning is async: Commerce publishes Kafka `commerce.user-creation-requested.v1` with the password **AES-256-GCM field-encrypted**; Identity `UserCreationRequestedConsumer` decrypts+creates. **DRIFT:** Commerce has `FieldEncryption.Key` in its `appsettings.Development.json` (`caLuf…FqeAE=`) but **Identity had NO key** (`appsettings.json` default `""`) → `[CODE] AesGcmFieldEncryptor.cs:25` throws `FieldEncryption key must be exactly 32 bytes … Got 0 bytes` at consumer `:140` (eager resolve, before idempotency `:163`) → user never created. Seeds bypass this (direct insert) so only the UI path breaks; consumer error path doesn't commit offset → reprocesses on restart. **FIX (no app source touched — user forbade appsettings edit): created `C:\Falcon\Falcon\Falcon\docker-compose.override.yml` with identity `FieldEncryption__Key` = Commerce's key; `docker compose -f docker-compose.yml -f docker-compose.override.yml up -d --no-deps identity`.** Proof: post-restart consumer constructs encryptor OK, reprocessed test5 → idempotent skip, AND auto-provisioned a different stuck client (`asdasdasdaasdas`) end-to-end. **Seeded `test5`/`Admin@1234`** (acc-owner, tenant `6a2538c9e0119e8bd142c79a`) via new `falcon-essentials/zitadel/seed-test5-owner.sh` (single owner on existing tenant); login `POST :7777/api/auth/login`→stage 4. Order: seed BEFORE restart so idempotency keeps the known pwd. NO commits.](project_addclient_owner_kafka_fieldencryption_drift_2026_06_07.md) — 🟢 FIXED + RUNTIME-PROVEN 2026-06-07 (claude). Surfaced on admin Add-Contract Step-1 **Value** field + rate-card Price Value. **(1) No red on blur:** the `falcon-angular-input-number` wrapper swallowed Stencil `(falcon-blur)` internally (`handleBlur`→`onTouched` only) and exposed NO public `@Output` (unlike date-picker's `falconBlur`), so a field bound via standalone `[ngModel]`+`(valueChange)` (NOT formControlName/CVA) couldn't mark its reactive control touched → empty required Value stayed un-red until Next. FIX: added shared `@Output() falconBlur` + emit in `handleBlur()`; wire `(falconBlur)="onInfoFieldBlur(field)"`. **(2) Double red:** `[state]="error"` already paints the wrapper `bg-[var(--falcon-input-bg-error)]`; an extra `!bg-falcon-red-100` on the inner input STACKS a 2nd red. RULE: standard fields use ONLY `[state]`; `!bg-falcon-red-100` is wallet-drawer-only (custom `--falcon-wallet-*` tokens). **(3) ﷼/SAR overlay drops to input bottom on error:** the absolute glyphs center `top-1/2` against the `.relative` wrapper; passing `[errorMessage]` renders the error `<p>` INSIDE that wrapper → grows it → overlays drop. FIX = proven wallet-drawer pattern: never pass `[errorMessage]` on an overlay-centered input-number; render it as an EXTRACTED sibling `<p data-falcon-input role="alert">` BELOW `.relative`, reproducing `.falcon-input-error` tokens (`data-falcon-input` marker MANDATORY for `--falcon-input-error-*` scope). Principle: `.relative` = control-row only; label (above) + error (below) are siblings OUTSIDE it. Deploy = rebuild FE Docker image.](reference_input_number_overlay_validation_blur_doublered_iconcenter_2026_06_07.md) — 🟢 FIXED + BUILD-GREEN (admin+mgmt) 2026-06-07 (claude); runtime in-app pending FE rebuild + user click-test. - [**"Click a contract input → it disappears" ROOT-CAUSED** (2026-06-07, claude, CODE-ONLY; user declined live login, NO code changed). Editable contract inputs = light-DOM Stencil web components; the ones in `<falcon-angular-data-table>` (Rate Card + Contract Details matrix tabs) inject via **"Strategy E"** — `[CODE] falcon-table-tw.tsx:557/612` emits `falcon-cells-mounted` every render → wrapper `m.element.replaceChildren(...rootNodes)` (`falcon-data-table.component.ts:1009/1020`) DETACHES+RE-ATTACHES the focused `<input>` → focus lost / field "disappears" (codebase already names it "cell disappears as you type"). App is **zoneless** so a bare click triggers NO re-projection (rate-card/matrix keep `[data]` ref-stable on edit). **CONFIRMED trigger = Contract Details matrix `(focusout)→onCellBlur→invalidCells` signal → `matrixRows()` rebuilds a FRESH `[data]` array → full re-projection yanks the next-clicked cell** (introduced by `ccb96f0f` "bake validation into row data"). ## FE UI-Core Wrappers / Stencil (tabs registration race)
- [**Tab strip renders BLANK on first navigation after a build** (contract Details + every tabs surface, BOTH consoles) — ROOT-CAUSED + FIXED 2026-06-07 (claude). The strip is `<falcon-angular-tabs>`→`<falcon-tabs-tw>` (Stencil light-DOM), registered LAZILY via `void defineFalconTwComponent('falcon-tabs')` in the wrapper's ngOnInit (`[CODE] falcon-tabs.component.ts:69`); `main.ts` removed the eager `defineCustomElements()` warmup so EVERY wrapper races on cold load. On the FIRST nav after a build (chunk not yet downloaded) Angular renders `<falcon-tabs-tw [tabs]=…>` before the element is defined; the late upgrade re-applies Stencil's default `@Prop() tabs=[]` (`falcon-tabs.tsx:53`) → `render()` maps `[]` → ZERO tab buttons (page CONTENT still shows because `activeTab` defaults + body is a separate `@switch`, matching the user screenshot). 2nd visit = chunk already loaded → element upgrades synchronously → binding sticks ("go back and again it shows"). `falcon-stepper`/`falcon-data-table`/`falcon-dropdown` ALL already fix this with a `componentOnReady()` deferred imperative push; tabs was the lone array-prop wrapper missing it. FIX (1 shared file, fixes all tabs surfaces): keep `defineReady` promise + add `syncProps()` (awaits defineReady→componentOnReady→imperatively assigns tabs/selectedValue/mode/size/orientation/ariaLabel/helper/error), wired to ngAfterViewInit+ngOnChanges+handleChange; KEPT declarative bindings (warm-load path untouched, lowest-regression); NO HTML change. BOTH consoles `nx build` EXIT0 with fresh falcon-ui-core rebuild (mgmt Hash 7aa4a9ee4fb8c344 / admin 839b168b264b53dc), zero warnings on falcon-tabs.component.ts. NO commits, live login pending (credential policy). **CHEAT-SHEET: any `<falcon-angular-*>` wrapper feeding a COMPLEX ARRAY prop purely via declarative `[prop]` → blank/empty on first cold load after build → cure = defineReady+componentOnReady deferred syncProps push.**](project_falcon_tabs_blank_strip_coldload_registration_race_2026_06_07.md) — 🟢 ROOT-CAUSED + BUILD-VERIFIED (admin+mgmt) 2026-06-07 (claude), polishing-v0.4. Related [[reference_static_remote_rebuild_after_app_edit_2026_06_04]] · [[reference_fe_structure_standard_angular21_2026_06_02]].

## Contracts "input disappears as you type" — TRUE root cause (corrects prior slot-wipe diagnosis)
- [**CORRECTED root cause 2026-06-06 (claude + ammar-qa-web):** the admin Add/Edit-Contract "input disappears as you type" is **NOT** the Stencil shadow:false icon-SLOT wipe that was repeatedly mis-fixed (slot-less conversion) — that's why it kept recurring. PROOF slot-wipe is wrong: all contract templates are already slot-less in source AND the running `dist/apps/admin-console` bundle was built 9:01 PM (AFTER the ≤7:44 PM slot-less edits) with slot-less markers present, yet still disappears. TRUE CAUSE = the Falcon **data-table Strategy-E cell re-projection firing on EVERY keystroke** (rate-card + matrix ONLY, both consoles): cell `(valueChange)`→`model.set(freshArray)` + a `mountKick` `effect` that bumps on every model change → `matrixRows()`/`viewRows()` emit a new `[data]` array each keystroke → `syncProps` sets `el.rows=newRef` → Stencil re-render → `componentDidRender` re-emits `falcon-cells-mounted` → wrapper `mountOrReuseView` runs `detectChanges()` (re-pushes controlled `[ngModel]` mid-type) + `m.element.replaceChildren(...)` which RE-PARENTS the focused `<input>` → blur/flicker/"disappears". Matrix `onCellChange` has NO value-equality guard (worst); rate-card `onPriceValueChange` does. DISCRIMINATOR (source-verified): data-table inputs (matrix cell, rate-card Price Value) BREAK; non-table inputs (Step-1 Value, Addons cards) are FINE — same `<falcon-angular-input-number>`, so the projection cycle is the culprit. Dev's own comment `rate-card-step.html:73-75` admits "every keystroke produces a new viewRows() row → the cell re-projects". Regular `data-cell-mount` cells inject straight into the `<td>`; shadow cells use a stable Stencil-keyed `<div data-shadow-mount>` (the fix shadow rows already got). FIX A (contained): make rate-card+matrix `[data]`/`[columns]` referentially stable during value edits + gate mountKick to structural-only. FIX B (library root-cause): give regular cells the stable mount wrapper. ⚠️ LIVE VERIFY BLOCKED (Claude-in-Chrome extension disconnected; computer-use read-tier can't type) — needs the extension re-paired. NOT yet fixed/committed.](project_contracts_input_disappears_datatable_reprojection_rootcause_2026_06_06.md) — 🔴 ROOT-CAUSED (source-convergent), FIX PENDING USER CHOICE + live verify 2026-06-06. Related [[project_contracts_matrix_falcon_datatable_migration_2026_06_06]] · [[reference_fe_structure_standard_angular21_2026_06_02]].

So `[state]="cellState()"`/signal-read INSIDE the cell NEVER re-evaluates → red never shows (FAILED #1: built + 11 unit tests green but DEAD at runtime — unit tests calling the method directly can't catch it). Native `:user-invalid`+SCSS+`::ng-deep` (FAILED #2) rejected by user — ZERO such patterns in the codebase = structure violation. **FIX:** `invalidCells=signal<ReadonlySet>` (`priority::destination`); `matrixRows()` reads it (tracked) + bakes `cellErrors:{[dest]:bool}` per flattened row (matrix VALUES read `untracked` → NOT recomputed per keystroke → preserves disappears-as-you-type invariant; deps = structureKey/mountKick/invalidCells only); cell `[state]="row.cellErrors?.[dest.destination] ? 'error':'default'"` + `(focusout)="onCellBlur(...)"` (add key if empty / remove if filled / else NO-OP so no needless re-projection; `<td key={col.key}>` keyed → reused → no re-parent → focus kept on tab). Reset in `changeRateSelection`. RED-only (no per-cell text — 11 dynamic cols too noisy; Add-Client shows text bc only 2 editable cols). ADMIN-only editable (Add+Edit share `contract-details-step`); mgmt mirrored for byte-parity (inert, view-only). NO `libs/**` change, Falcon components only. Verified: admin vitest 784/784 (matrix spec +9→28) + mgmt 617/617 (section spec +7→26); admin+mgmt `nx build --configuration=development` EXIT 0 (admin `133a597b595189f0`, mgmt `5f9ef967d35c72ce`); my files lint-clean (admin lint target fails ONLY on PRE-EXISTING untracked `old-test-charging` `*ngIf`/`*ngFor`, NOT mine). ⚠️ live pixel-verify pending login but HIGH confidence (identical mechanism to shipped, user-confirmed Add-Client validation). - [falcon-ui-core build **FatalProcessOutOfMemory + EMFILE** (broke host-shell/admin/mgmt builds — single shared dep on every app's `^build` path) ROOT CAUSE = unbounded AGGREGATE CSS-compile cost: `maxConcurrentWorkers:4` (was raised 1→4) × ~2–2.5GB/compile Tailwind-v4 (native `@tailwindcss/oxide` scan of 110 .tsx, run per-component for the 36 comps that `@import tailwind.css`) → 8–10GB heap + 4× native-FD burst on constrained Linux CI (`core dumped`, ulimit-n 1024). graceful-fs CAN'T help (Oxide file I/O is native, bypasses Node fs). build.cjs is sync (NO Promise.all); guard `verifyNoStubsRemain` was the messenger. FIXED 2026-06-04: `stencil.config.ts` memory-aware `resolveMaxWorkers()` (freeMem÷~2GB/compile, clamp cores, floor1/ceil4, `STENCIL_MAX_WORKERS` override, scales UP to 4 when RAM free / DOWN when constrained) + `build.cjs` invalidate `.stencil` on failure + `distIndexIsReal()` (size>0, seeder writes 0-byte index) sentinel. KEPT guard/graceful-fs/UV_THREADPOOL/`cache:false`. 2 files, byte-identical CSS. **COMMITTED+PUSHED commit `0718c57a` → origin/polishing-v0.4 (scoped to 6 build-tooling files; concurrent contracts/﷼ work left uncommitted); spec table embedded in build.cjs header.**](reference_falcon_ui_core_build_oom_emfile_fix_2026_06_04.md) — 🟢 FIXED+BUILD-VERIFIED+PUSHED 2026-06-04 (claude): nx build falcon-ui-core + run-many host-shell/admin/mgmt all EXIT 0, 0 OOM/EMFILE/stub, 106 real defineCustomElement, adaptive proven (5.3GB→2,7.7GB→3,10.2GB→4 workers). ⚠️ verifying needs the live `nx serve host-shell` MF stack STOPPED (corrupts static remotes); restart `npm start`. - [Uploader water-fill animation FIXED 2026-05-30 (Phase 9, post-port) — switched water from `height: var(--fu-progress)%` (which computes to 0 on flex-content absolute children per CSS spec) to `clip-path: inset(calc(100% - var(--fu-progress)) 0 0 0)` set as INLINE Stencil JSX style (Tailwind v4 silently drops arbitrary values with commas inside var() fallbacks); added animated upload lifecycle to showcase lab (60ms ticks, uploadDuration default 2s, 1.5s floor); browser-verified water rises 0→100% smoothly with pill ticks "0% → 27% → 51% → 87% → Completed"](project_uploader_animation_fix_clippath_2026_05_30.md) — 🟢 BROWSER-VERIFIED on host-shell dev preview port 4200, NO COMMITS, branch polishing-v0.4. Two compounding bugs: (1) percentage HEIGHT on absolute children of flex-content rows resolves to 0 (parent "indefinite" for child %-height even when content drives concrete px). (2) Tailwind v4 rejects arbitrary values like `h-[var(--fu-progress,0%)]`, `transition-[clip-path,background-color]`, `animate-[fuLaserPulse_1.2s_ease-in-out_infinite]` — `@source inline()` + `[property:value]` syntax both ignored. **FIX**: `file-uploader.tw-layout.tsx` twWaterLayer water mode emits `style={{ clipPath: 'inset(calc(100% - var(--fu-progress,0%)) 0 0 0)', transition: 'clip-path 450ms cubic-bezier(0.22,1,0.36,1), background-color 0.3s' }}`. clip-path % resolves against own border-box (always definite). Same fix in `file-uploader.shadow.css`. Bar+laser branches keep width-based approach (width % works fine). **NEW USER-FACING** lab: `uploadDuration` signal default 2s + slider; `fireAnimatedUpload()` setInterval 60ms; on 100% flip to 'success'; 1.5s floor = `Math.max(1500, …)`. New primary button "Animated upload (Ns)" + renamed "Snap 27%/67%". **BROWSER-VERIFIED** at t=50/550/1050/1750/2550ms: clipPath 100% → 78.28% → 53.07% → 17.99% → 0%, pill ticks correctly, status flips to 'success'. Snap sweep all 6 (water/bar/laser × image/document) PASS — water 92px×377px clip-path inset(73%); bar 4px×102px; laser 2px×102px+14px glow. **RESIDUAL**: laser fuLaserPulse keyframe animation shows `animName:none` despite inline `animation:'fuLaserPulse 1.2s ...'` — Stencil JSX shorthand+transformOrigin edge case (glow renders, only pulse missing); reduced-motion gate for inline animation needs JS/scoped-style follow-up. 5 files changed. Builds on [[project_uploader_document_show_port_2026_05_30]]. · 2026-05-30

## Feedback — Standing Rules
- [ALWAYS Falcon custom components, NEVER native HTML (tables/inputs/selects); tables = `<falcon-angular-data-table>` + `[paginator]` (pattern: client-applications-step — ColumnDef[] + `*falconDataTableCell` + afterNextRender mount-kick). data-table gaps: single-row expansion only; dynamic editable cols need `ColumnDef.template` (shared TemplateRef, ctx {row,field,rowIndex,value}).](feedback_falcon_no_native_html_use_components_2026_06_06.md) — 🔴 user mandate 2026-06-06
- [Never modify code or commit](feedback_never_modify_code_or_commit_2026_05_20.md) — 🔴 RULE · Never edit source, never commit, never even ask. All fix proposals go into a separate report file under `reports/` · 2026-05-20
- [PR review silent by default](feedback_pr_review_silent_default.md) — 🔴 RULE · PR reviews post nothing to the PR; silent review default; always ask before any PR comment · 2026-05-19
- [API code stays in host app](feedback_api_code_stays_in_host_app.md) — 🔴 RULE · API-calling services live in host-shell, never the library; library is presentation-only · 2026-05-19
- [Data table page size 10](feedback_data_table_default_page_size_10.md) — 🔴 RULE · Every Falcon data table defaults to page size 10 · 2026-05-18
- [Test-user password standard](feedback_test_user_password_standard.md) — 🔴 RULE · Every test/seed user in every Falcon env uses password `Admin@1234` · 2026-05-16
- [PES g-link uses Zitadel id](feedback_pes_g_link_uses_zitadel_id.md) — 🔴 RULE · PES g-rule `obj` must be `u:<ZitadelUserId>@<ns>`, never MongoObjectId; else silent deny · 2026-05-16
- [Orchestrator failure modes](feedback_orchestrator_failure_modes_org_hierarchy.md) — 🔴 RULE · 10 failure patterns + 10 locked rules. Read before any UI parity task · 2026-05-15
- [Falcon library mandatory](feedback_falcon_custom_library_mandatory.md) — 🔴 RULE · Falcon library FIRST; strict customization order; Tailwind+tokens only · 2026-05-15
- [Library-only, no native UI](feedback_falcon_ui_library_only_no_native.md) — 🔴 RULE · Only falcon-ui-core angular-wrapper; zero native interactive elements in apps/ · 2026-05-16
- [Skeleton + app-wrapper layers](feedback_library_skeleton_app_api.md) — 🔴 RULE · Two layers: library skeleton (presentational) + app wrapper (injects services) · 2026-05-15
- [No external API dependency](feedback_no_external_api_dependency.md) — 🔴 RULE · Never push for ChatGPT/Gemini keys; Claude + local files is enough · 2026-05-18
- [Discard old UI repos](feedback_discard_old_ui.md) — Always exclude falcon-web-platform-ui-old and deprecated-falcon-web-platform-ui
- [Canonical Falcon frontend repo](feedback_webstorm_duplicate_workspace.md) — 🔴 RULE · Frontend repo is `C:\Falcon\Falcon\falcon-web-platform-ui` (the git working copy) — always · 2026-05-19
- [Self-explore, don't ask](feedback_self_explore.md) — Never ask user which path to take; pick the most interesting option and explore autonomously
- [Auth via Identity Service](feedback_frontend_auth_identity_service.md) — Frontend NEVER calls Zitadel directly; all auth goes through Identity Service
- [Wiki naming conventions](feedback_wiki_naming_conventions.md) — All Nx apps/libs/paths follow Wiki kebab-case + @falcon/* scope rules
- [Tailwind grid-first](feedback_tailwind_grid_first.md) — Tailwind CSS Grid is the default layout primitive; flexbox only for small inline alignment
- [No inline styles, tokens only](feedback_no_inline_styles_tokens_only.md) — Zero inline styles, tokens only; pre-finish grep gate codified
- [Shadow tokens are SSOT](feedback_shadow_is_token_ssot.md) — Stencil Shadow + `<name>.tokens.css` are SSOT; Tailwind variant mirrors same tokens
- [No UI testing while building](feedback_no_ui_testing_during_implementation.md) — No dev-serve/preview/browser verification during implementation; separate user-initiated phase
- [Strict task scope](feedback_strict_task_scope.md) — Never edit infra/config/tooling outside task scope; stop and report broken external files
- [Angular-only scope](feedback_angular_only_scope.md) — LOCKED 2026-05-08 · Falcon UI Wave 3+ targets Angular wrapper only; skip React + Vue
- [Always build zero errors](feedback_always_build_zero_errors.md) — After non-trivial changes, run `nx build <app>` and fix every error before reporting done
- [Build must be green](feedback_build_must_be_green.md) — No phase ships red; dispatch a fix agent immediately on any `nx build` error
- [Clean, DRY, minimal code](feedback_clean_code_dry_minimal.md) — Minimum code, clean, DRY, idiomatic; no duplication, no speculative abstractions
- [Terse comment style](feedback_comment_style.md) — Terse banner-format comments only (`*** ... ***` delimited, max 2 lines), no verbose JSDoc
- [Folder-structure pattern](feedback_folder_structure_pattern.md) — One file per type-folder (models/models.ts, services/services.ts, etc.)
- [`--` spawns parallel agent](feedback_double_dash_immediate_parallel.md) — Messages prefixed `--` spawn an agent immediately in parallel; strip the `--` before briefing
- [Beep when input needed](feedback_beep_when_input_needed.md) — Long beep ONLY when user input is required to proceed; never on routine actions
- [Orchestrator voice](feedback_orchestrator_voice.md) — Speak as orchestrator only; hide agent mechanics/IDs/briefs; concise outcome-focused replies
- [Never push without permission](feedback_never_push_without_explicit_permission.md) — Never `git push`/open PRs unless user explicitly says "push"
- [Never commit without permission](feedback_never_commit_without_explicit_permission.md) — Never `git commit` unless user explicitly says "commit"
- [No commit/push strict](feedback_no_commit_no_push_strict_2026_05_02.md) — STRICT · Never commit/push without "commit"/"push" in the CURRENT message
- [Brain skills protocol](feedback_brain_skills_protocol.md) — Brain skills at `C:\falcon\brain-skills\`; trigger phrases, cross-skill flow, sound signatures
- [Skill status announcer](feedback_skill_status_announcer.md) — Skill activation/completion includes voice-spoken status + "I am finishing, boss." handshake
- [Voice lifecycle hooks](feedback_voice_lifecycle_hooks.md) — Prompt/Notification/Stop/PostToolUse hooks route to Brain MP3 voices via voice-hook.ps1
- [Brain Generated folder](feedback_brain_generated_folder.md) — All Brain-generated output lives under `C:\falcon\Brain\Brain Generated\`
- [API analysis folder pattern](feedback_api_analysis_folder_pattern.md) — API analysis uses app/controller/endpoint folder hierarchy with per-endpoint md files
- [Brain skill (tri-mindset)](feedback_brain_skill.md) — Brain skill at `C:\falcon\Brain\` (ChatGPT/Claude/Gemini); each mindset its own voice
- [Night-mode trigger](feedback_night_mode_trigger.md) — Trigger phrases load deferred specs from `C:\falcon\Brain\jobs\` and run pre-approved checklists
- [Orchestrator full permission](feedback_orchestrator_full_permission.md) — Adnan has full orchestrator authority for the Brain pipeline (autonomous, parallel agents)
- [Brain canonical path](feedback_brain_canonical_path.md) — Falcon Brain canonical root is `C:\falcon\Brain`
- [Brain banner on session start](feedback_brain_banner_session_start.md) — Falcon Brain banner auto-prints on every Claude Code SessionStart via PS hook
- [Claude launch wrapper](feedback_claude_launch_wrapper.md) — `$PROFILE` `claude` function prints Brain banner before launching Claude Code
- [list/ls command trigger](feedback_list_ls_command_trigger.md) — `list`/`ls`/`/list`/`/ls` trigger the available-commands panel mid-session
- [Noor Instructions skill](feedback_noor_instructions.md) — Always-on skill for Admin Console UI; 8 categories; overrides angular-tailwind on color naming
- [Brain SK dismissed alerts](feedback_brain_sk_dismissed_alerts.md) — 2026-05-13 · Two Brain SK HIGH gaps explicitly dismissed (GAP-001/002/003); do not re-surface
